<p dir="auto">🔭 I’m currently working on :-<br>
Building backend-first full-stack projects 🧠, experimenting with document analysis and clean dark-mode UI 📄🌙, while sharpening my DSA and competitive programming skills in C++ ⚙️.<br><br></p>
<p dir="auto">👯 I’m looking to collaborate on :-<br>
Hackathon projects where logic beats flashy UI 🚀, real-world web apps with solid backend flows 🔗, and practical AI or automation tools that actually solve problems 🤖.<br><br></p>
<p dir="auto">🤝 I’m looking for help with :-<br>
Designing scalable backend architectures 🏗️, writing production-quality code instead of quick fixes 🧩, and understanding system design without buzzwords 🧠.<br><br></p>
<p dir="auto">🌱 I’m currently learning :-<br>
Backend development with Node.js and Express 🔥, strengthening frontend fundamentals before jumping to React 🎨, and mastering DSA in C++ with a logic-first mindset ⚙️.<br><br></p>
<p dir="auto">💬 Ask me about :-<br>
C++ logic mistakes, competitive programming breakdowns 🧠, and how backend servers handle requests from start to finish 🌐.<br><br></p>
<p dir="auto">⚡ Fun fact :-<br>
If I can’t explain why a piece of code works, I don’t trust it 🤨.</p>
